# Numbered results: source inventory and audit status

This inventory is in progress. It does not certify a numbered result, authorize a frozen declaration, or count proofs. `OPEN` means the indicated check remains incomplete; `NOT YET CHECKED` is not a finding of absence. The candidate and provenance columns are leads requiring source, binder, definition and use checks.

Source: `paper/lqg_harmonic_ball-corrected.tex`, SHA-256 `e3ed40660ed77327b991b2463b599cc2a56426fff8819a57b3f4c441c2680b15`. This inventory contains 78 results and six definitions, including Lemmas 11.18–11.24. Lines refer to that source. The original inventory and its detailed historical receipts are available in Git at commit `3e2bc627e10ffa351cb541b5f2dbde80fd5b497f` for this same path.

The source, binder, definition, consumer and transitive verdicts below are independent. A blank or unexamined cell does not imply success. Kind labels and legacy candidates are preliminary; a cited proof input and the paper’s own result may coexist in one row. All candidate binder and axiom checks are NOT YET CHECKED except where stated explicitly below. A cell reading `ACCEPTED (scoped, 2026-11-17)` or `ACCEPTED (scoped, 2026-11-18)` records the source acceptance completed on that date for the declaration named in the cell, together with every scope limitation of that acceptance. A cell beginning `PARTIAL (scoped, 2026-11-18)`, `INTERNAL (scoped, 2026-11-18)` or `NONE (scoped, 2026-11-18)` records a completed consumer check of that date whose finding is, respectively, use by only some of the printed consumers or by unconsumed chains, use only inside the candidate's own proof chain, or no consumer; a cell beginning `OPEN:` names what remains. A source cell beginning `WEAKER-SCOPE (scoped, 2026-11-18)` records a completed source check whose candidate proves only the stated special instance of the printed result; the general printed form remains OPEN. When a row names a faithful form other than the listed candidate, the cell gives the faithful form first and states the listed candidate's own scope. In rows 2.1, 2.2, 2.5 and 2.6, a clause beginning `cited statement` records, after the candidates' verdict, the source acceptance of 2026-11-18 of a closed Prop transcribing the cited fact or cited theorem; such a Prop is stated, not proved, and its truth rests on the cited literature. A receipt clause `type recheck at HEAD 5b86f000` followed by a run id records the typed recheck of 2026-11-18, at that commit, of the module, line, type and standard axioms of every declaration the row names in columns 5 and 6; it replaces the earlier type-receipt ids of the row. All other cells keep their earlier status.

## Corrected Lemma 10.5 and geometry checks

The corrected source at lines 2674–2679 states a deterministic finite-crossing lemma. `firstPass_10_5_corrected` takes a nonempty bounded open connected planar set `G`, a finite exceptional set and arbitrarily small positive radii with finitely many components of `G ∩ ball p (2*r)` whose closures meet both stated spheres. It concludes local connectedness of `closure G`, absence of cut points and simple-loop boundaries for its complement components. It also takes `TorhorstSimpleLoopFrozen`; `firstPass_10_5_corrected_derivedInputs46` supplies `torhorstSimpleLoopFrozen_holds`. The latter is obtained through definitional bridges to a Lattice-Probability theorem. Strict source checks of the geometry definitions, corrected producer and wrapper passed; a full typed application of the wrapper and standard axiom checks passed. In `FirstPass/S1S10.lean:124`, the 10.6 candidate applies the original producer with the exceptional set `{0}`, retaining its own crossing, cluster-openness and Torhorst inputs. This is a checked source application, not a verdict that the 10.6 source or its inputs are discharged.

For the definitions used by 10.5, `CrossesAnnulus` explicitly asks the closure to meet each sphere and `crossingComponents` selects connected components meeting that condition; `IsLocallyConnectedSet` asks for connected relative neighborhoods; `IsCutPoint` uses non-preconnectedness after removal and retains membership. The source restricts the cut-point definition to compact connected locally connected sets. A strict Lean check proves equivalence with membership and a nonconnected remainder when the remainder is nonempty; the empty-remainder convention and full source equivalence remain OPEN. The definition bodies and their standard axiom sets, the no-cut-point application, and an explicit Torhorst application are checked in `wlc-hb-183837-813929`. This check does not establish complete downstream usage or transitive source correspondence. `IsSimpleLoop` is an injective continuous circle image. Its bridge, and the local-connectivity and cut-point bridges, to the corresponding Lattice-Probability definitions are definitional. The source meaning of the arc parameter in 10.2, every definition-body equivalence, the Torhorst source correspondence and the complete transitive provenance remain OPEN. Row 10.5 of the table records the checks of 2026-11-18 for the corrected lemma: the cut-point convention equals the printed one because the closure is nontrivial, the Torhorst Prop is equivalent to its standard form and keeps only the simple-loop clause the proof uses, and the literature text of the cited form is not yet read. Rows 10.2-10.4 of the table record the source acceptance of 2026-11-18, which settles the definition items of this paragraph: the arc parameter reading (arcs have no consumer), the equivalence of `IsLocallyConnectedSet` with the printed form and with `LocallyConnectedSpace`, and the cut-point convention, which differs from the printed one only for a singleton, a case every consumer excludes.

For 11.1, Lean allows any real linear isometry in the local graph representation, and the exact interpretation of the paper's coordinate reorientation remains OPEN. For 11.3, `IsCone` permits an open sector or the complement of one. For 11.4, the predicates place the boundary condition on their use rather than in the predicate. The typed application of `FirstPassS11.isLipschitzDomain_satisfiesConeCondition` at a boundary point and its standard axioms were checked. The Lipschitz, sector, cone and cone-condition bodies and standard axiom sets were printed in `wlc-hb-184514-846546`. These checks do not establish full definition equivalence; the complete source and consumer audit for 11.2 and 11.5 remains OPEN. The source acceptance recorded in rows 11.1, 11.3 and 11.4 of the table (2026-11-18) settles these definition items: the reading of the coordinate reorientation by every linear isometry is the one the print needs (remark at :2877-2878), and the cone and cone-condition predicates equal the print by Iff.rfl transcriptions. Rows 11.2 and 11.5 carry their own source acceptance.

## Checked declarations and remaining questions

- The 12 candidates `HarmonicBalls.Closure.closure_6_n` for 6.1–6.4 and 6.6–6.13 in `FirstPass/Closure/Sec6.lean:37-87` each elaborate with the same three leading inputs: `∀ Ω [MeasurableSpace Ω] P γ hC hcirc, WholePlaneLQGBallMassBounds P γ hC hcirc`; `∀ Ω [MeasurableSpace Ω] P γ hC hcirc, LQGMeasureCoordChange P γ 0 hC hcirc`; and `∀ Ω [MeasurableSpace Ω] P γ α hC hcirc, LQGMeasureExistence P γ α hC hcirc`. These inputs are defined in `Proposed/LQG.lean:172,131,74` respectively. For 6.12 the conclusion is `ShellDecompositionStmt`, but it retains all three outer inputs. The output of `#check @HarmonicBalls.Closure.closure_6_n` and `#print axioms HarmonicBalls.Closure.closure_6_n` was checked for each listed n after importing `HarmonicBalls.FirstPass.Closure.Sec6`; each printed axiom set is `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. This checks outer binders and kernel axiom dependencies only. The inner statements, Proposed-input source correspondence, actual downstream consumers and transitive semantics remain OPEN. The historical header-based zero counts are not elaborated binder counts.
- For 6.12, `HarmonicBalls.shellDecompositionStmt_proved : ShellDecompositionStmt` (`Proofs/Sec6/ShellDecomposition.lean:164`) has no outer assumptions. Its source passes a warning-as-error check. `#check` and `#print axioms` after importing `HarmonicBalls.Proofs.Sec6.DerivedInputAssembly` give the same conclusion and `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]` for this producer and the actual wrapper `Section6Derived.shellDecompositionStmt_of_proved`, which applies it at `Proofs/Sec6/DerivedInputAssembly.lean:98-99`. `Draft/Section6.lean:170-188,442-449` expresses the shell radii, widths, scale and three properties of the source at lines 1712–1750. For positive shell radii, the shell equals the frontier of the corresponding open ball plus the width ball; the producer bounds imply these radii are positive. For a positive inner radius, `annulus` equals the outer open ball minus the closure of the inner ball; `shellScale` reduces to `(2⁻¹)^(N₀+j)`. These identities and radius positivity passed a strict check, together with `#check` and `#print axioms` for `Section6.section6_of_volumeGrowth`; its conjunction directly contains the producer at `Proofs/Sec6/Section6FromVolumeGrowth.lean:54` and retains volume growth, affine invariance, coordinate change and existence inputs. Both the producer and that conjunction have only `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. Full source correspondence, broader consumers and transitive review remain OPEN. The older `closure_6_12` still has its three elaborated inputs.
- `HarmonicBalls.greenPotentialHolderStmt_holds.{u} : GreenPotentialHolderStmt.{u}` has no outer premises. Its direct source check has no warnings, and its axiom set is `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. Two exact typed uses in `FirstPass/Phase2/DerivedInputs44.lean:34-39,69-73` and their standard axioms were checked. This addresses only the Green-potential clause of 2.5, not its LBM assertion. The binder review of that clause is provisional pending standing-definition review. Identification with the killed-Brownian occupation kernel is proved for the formal smooth bounded domain carrier as described below; its correspondence to the paper’s smooth-domain carrier and the transitive source semantics remain OPEN.
- Proposition 2.3 states positivity throughout the ball at paper lines 486–494. `Draft/Section2.lean:68-75` states positivity away from the pole, while `Defs/Green.lean:70-73` assigns zero at the pole. `FirstPass/S2S3S4S9SA/GreenPole.lean:29-36` adds a punctured limit at infinity to the earlier statement; `Closure.closure_2_3_withPole` exports this conjunction without outer premises. Both types and standard axiom sets were checked in `wlc-hb-181531-677176`; strict source check `wlc-hb-181542-678075` passed. `Proofs/Sec8/GreenKernelContinuity.lean:41` also gives continuity of an extended nonnegative kernel, but its equivalence to the paper’s Brownian Green function is OPEN. The exact application of `closure_2_3` in `FirstPass/Phase2/DerivedInputs39.lean:139-145` was retyped with all other inputs in `wlc-hb-181556-679005`; complete consumers and transitive provenance remain OPEN.
- At all universes `HarmonicBalls.correctedLemma11_13_forallXi_derived : CorrectedLemma11_13Stmt`, `HarmonicBalls.firstPass_11_11_forallXi_derived : HarmonicBallNotMetricBallStmt`, and `HarmonicBalls.firstPass_1_2_forallXi_derived : HarmonicBallNoveltyStmt` have no outer premises; their file `FirstPass/Phase2/WeylExponentOfDG20.lean:72,76,80` passes the direct warning-as-error check, and each has exactly `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]` as its printed axiom set. Source correspondence, inner definitions, actual applications and transitive semantic review remain OPEN. The older conditional `hStep8` forms remain conditional; a later declaration does not change their types.
- The corrected 11.13, older `ManyPinchScalesStmt`, and canonical-exponent form have distinct geometry or scope and are not identified by this inventory. Separate canonical candidates are `correctedLemma11_13Canonical_derived`, `harmonicBallNotMetricBallCanonical_derived`, and `harmonicBallNoveltyCanonical_derived` in `FirstPass/Phase2/LocalMetricReplacementDerivedCanonical.lean:24,28,32`; their current elaborated binders, sources, consumers and axioms are NOT YET CHECKED here.
- `step8Identification_derived : Step8IdentificationStmt.{u}` in `FirstPass/Phase2/Step8IdentificationProved.lean` has an exact typed joint-witness application and standard printed axioms. Its independent source, definition, downstream consumer and transitive semantic reviews remain OPEN.

- For 8.2, `firstPass_8_2_S8d_noWeyl_derivedInputsCampbell` has one elaborated outer input: for every probability space, field and circle-average family, `WholePlaneLQGBallMassBounds` holds. The statement of that input is in `Proposed/LQG.lean:172-178`. Its body invokes `firstPass_8_2_S8d_noWeyl_derivedInputs41` with `hSub := dmsLemmaA10WholePlaneMarkedSub_forall` and the retained `hDG`; this exact application was retyped. The candidate source passes a warning-as-error check, and its axiom set and that of the supplied marked identity are each `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. This checks the displayed application and outer binder, not the source correspondence, field normalization, or full transitive semantics. The frozen marked input (`FirstPass/Frozen/S11i.lean:59-67`) includes the subcritical range and measurable nonnegative marked tests; the unmarked Proposed input is distinct. The standalone abstract `MillerSheffieldIG4Prop29` has no other name occurrences in `HarmonicBalls` Lean files; that search does not rule out another implementation of the shift.
- `GreenOccupation.integral_occupation_exitTime_eq_logPotential` (`Proofs/Potential/GreenOccupationCharacterization.lean:225`) proves for bounded open `D`, `y ∈ D`, and smooth compactly supported `g` that Brownian occupation up to exit equals twice the logarithmic-potential difference between the starting point and the expected exit location. Its source at commit `1f19b5843ee92da07c505b6027a8fa29184958b1` passes warning-as-error checking and its axiom set is `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. It is a useful occupation formula, but the displayed statement does not identify `greenFunction` with the occupation density or establish Proposition 2.5's LBM assertion. The later smooth-domain measure characterization and normalization are described below; correspondence to the paper’s carrier remains OPEN.
- `GreenOccupation.integral_occupation_exitTime_eq_greenPotential` (`Proofs/Potential/GreenOccupationCharacterization.lean:324`, commit `9f903aa083057df2e0cfce8346369ded11b6fb92`) identifies ordinary planar Brownian occupation, for `x ∈ D` and a smooth compactly supported test whose support is in smooth bounded open `D`, with twice the Lebesgue Green potential. The committed source passed strict own-file checking; a full typed reapplication and standard three axioms were checked against a later live source snapshot adding an unrelated theorem and import. A later measure characterization extends the result to Borel sets on the same formal smooth-domain carrier; actual downstream consumers, transitive provenance, extension beyond that carrier, and relation to the LBM clock remain OPEN. This is not the LBM exit-time clause.


- `GreenOccupation.occupationMeasure_eq_greenMeasure` (`Proofs/Potential/GreenOccupationCharacterization.lean:721`, commit `8898b7f0fb9dd3323c901ef44320c06d5476f556`) states that the killed occupation measure equals planar volume with density `ENNReal.ofReal (2 * greenFunction D x ·)`, assuming `IsSmoothBoundedOpen D` and `x ∈ D`. `occupationMeasure_apply` and `lintegral_brownian_occupation_eq_green` at lines 733 and 741 give the measurable-set and Brownian-path forms. The committed source passes a warning-as-error check; the elaborated types and printed axiom sets of all three were checked and each has `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. The latter two are internal uses of the measure equality; no separate downstream use was found at that commit. The density is an almost-everywhere characterization with respect to volume, so the chosen diagonal value does not affect this measure. The formal smooth-domain carrier, full source correspondence, LBM clock, other consumers and transitive provenance remain OPEN.

- The six source definitions have corresponding bodies in `Defs/Geometry.lean`; these bodies were printed in Lean, while source equivalence is OPEN. For 10.2, arcs use `circleArc α β` with `α ≤ β < α + 2π`, and simple-arc injectivity is on that arc; the source's arc parameter notation needs separate interpretation. For 10.3, `IsLocallyConnectedSet` quantifies over relative neighborhoods and requires a connected relative neighborhood. For 10.4, `IsCutPoint` requires membership and non-preconnectedness after removal, while compactness, connectedness and local connectedness are carried by applications. For 11.1, the definition uses an arbitrary real linear isometry of the plane; equivalence to the source's coordinate language remains OPEN. For 11.3, `IsCone` allows an open sector or its complement. For 11.4, the boundary-point restriction is carried by applications, not the predicates. Candidate statement uses include `Draft/Section10.lean:108,122` and `Draft/Section11.lean:141,150`; their full elaborated consumer audits remain OPEN.

## Proposed inputs and source references

All eight modules `Proposed/Birkhoff.lean`, `Proposed/DuplantierMillerSheffield.lean`, `Proposed/GilbargTrudinger.lean`, `Proposed/LQG.lean`, `Proposed/LQGDimension.lean`, `Proposed/MetricProperness.lean`, `Proposed/MillerSheffieldIG4.lean`, and `Proposed/PfefferMetricScaling.lean` require full source and semantic review. Frozen bundles and their consumption are OPEN.

`gilbargTrudingerThm2_14_holds` (`Proofs/Potential/GilbargTrudingerDirichletProved.lean:27`, commit `26cfb505003bc2fdbf6cd8965ebd9aa0181fb1cf`) proves the existing `GilbargTrudingerThm2_14` with no outer hypotheses. An independently retyped application expands the exterior-sphere predicate: for each bounded connected open plane set, every boundary point has a positive-radius exterior ball with that point on its sphere and contained in the complement of the closure; continuous boundary data then have a continuous-on-closure harmonic extension. A separately retyped full application supplies this producer to `GreenGeneral.exists_dirichlet_holderOnWith` (`Proofs/Potential/GreenGeneralBoundary.lean:607-614`), preserving that consumer's other hypotheses. The producer and composed application have printed axiom sets `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`; the producer's own module build and strict source check passed. The consumer itself still takes the input explicitly. Its displayed conclusion asserts some positive Hölder exponent, while its docstring mentions the specific exponent `a/(a+2)`. The cited book wording, full consumer source correspondence, transitive semantic audit, and connection to the full Proposition 2.5 remain OPEN.

## Section 1 source and model gates

The corrected source at lines 136–146 uses signed Radon measures supported in `O \ D`. `Defs/HarmonicBall.lean:86-110` uses a difference of finite measures concentrated on one compact subset of `O \ D`. `GreenPotentialHolder` and the separate Radon test bridges address particular finite-measure and potential steps; the equivalence of the source support convention, relative support, possible boundary accumulation, diagonal and integrability with the formal test class remains OPEN. Green normalization and the Brownian occupation representative are separate OPEN gates. `HarmonicBallRadonTestBridge.lean` (commit `0ed69b7b`) has an independent formal review: its four signed and positive measure bridges passed strict checking, a module build, typed applications and standard-axiom checks. The signed theorem uses finite whole-plane signed measures with ambient variation support, and assumes an existing generalized harmonic ball; it does not settle relative support or source-class equivalence. No external consumer of the four exports was found in the reviewed snapshot. Source, definition, consumer, transitive and lead acceptance for these bridges remain OPEN. Theorem 1.1 retains its all-centres event, selected family and exact uniqueness obligations; 1.2 additionally retains the family, metric model, per-metric event and novelty predecessor obligations.

For 1.3, source lines 183–184 require the containment event itself to be measurable in the restricted-field sigma-algebra. `Draft/Section1.lean:151-165` instead provides an almost-everywhere equal measurable event, for an arbitrary family satisfying the family predicate almost surely. The relation to the raw source event, including null modifications and the reading of time zero, remains OPEN. The locality producer also uses measure locality and the existence and uniqueness predecessors; their source-to-consumer and transitive gates remain separate.
